`/root/autodl-tmp/models/pythia-1.4b/` | pad_token 已自带 |\\n| OLMo-1B | olmo | 1.18B | `q_proj,k_proj,v_proj` | `/root/autodl-tmp/models/olmo-1b-hf/` | 用 `allenai/OLMo-1B-hf`（非 OLMo-1B，后者需 hf_olmo 自定义代码）。BOS=None, PAD=1。**三个模块不是两个** |\\n| GPT-2 XL | gpt2 | 1.56B | `c_attn,c_proj` | `/root/autodl-tmp/models/gpt2-xl/` | 用 Conv1D 不是 nn.Linear，PEFT 自动处理。pad_token=None 需设为 eos_token |\\n| DeBERTa-v3-large | deberta-v2 | 434M | — | `/root/autodl-tmp/models/deberta-v3-large/` | DCOL end-to-end backbone。已下载验证。 |\\n\\n### 文本长度 confound 修复（Reviewer 2026-05-14）\\nmax_new_tokens=512，特征提取统一截断到 498 tokens。\\n\\n### GPU 硬件（2026-05-14）\\nwestb-52439 = autodl-rtx6000 = gpu-6000pro-2: 2× NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell 98GB。三个 hostname 是同一台机器。**Registry 和 cron 必须用系统已注册的服务器名**：`gpu-6000pro-2` 或 `autodl-rtx6000`。`westb-52439` 不在系统已知列表中，会导致 cron 监控 SSH 失败。\\n\\n## MVP 结果（2026-05-14, mvp_pipeline_pythia_002, SUCCESS）\\n\\n### 核心发现\\n- **4-class acc=78.2%** (chance=25%), 过滤代码后 81.6%（信号非域混淆）\\n- **3-class acc=70.8%** (d1/d2/d3 only, chance=33.3%)——去掉 depth-0 \\\"easy win\\\" 后信号依然强\\n- d0v1 AUC=0.998, d1v2=0.861, d2v3=0.801\\n- 3-class: d1v2=0.854, d2v3=0.793\\n- **最重要特征**: var_surprisal > p90_ppl > mean_ppl\\n- 训练 loss: 2.241→1.389→1.025（模式坍缩信号）\\n- 代码污染（宽标准）: d0=0.5%, d1=17.8%, d2=22.4%, d3=27.4%\\n- Stratified split 稳定: 78.2% → 78.1% (-0.1pp)\\n\\n### d1-d3 Per-class 分析（Dream 审稿约束，2026-05-14）\\n- **d2 是分类瓶颈**: d2 recall=60.7%, 主要被误判为 d3（279/1000 = 28%）。相邻深度间信号差递减，d2-d3 边界最模糊。\\n- Filtered 子集全面优于 Full（3-class acc 70.8% → 74.7%），证明信号来自深度本身而非 content-type confound。\\n- d1v3 AUC=0.957/0.977（filtered），跨 2 级深度区分近乎完美。\\n- DCOL ordinal loss 是改善 d2-d3 混淆的设计动机（利用序数约束惩罚相邻误判）。\\n\\n### 代码过滤标准差异\\n- 窄标准(4 patterns): d1=7.4%, d2=11.8%, d3=15.3%\\n- 宽标准(18 patterns): d1=17.8%, d2=22.4%, d3=27.4%\\n- 差异来自 def/function/var/div/php/SQL 等额外 pattern\\n\\n## 论文写作注意事项（Director/Reviewer 审查，2026-05-14）\\n- **CI 重叠处理**：temp09 [74.9,77.5] vs MVP [76.9,79.4] CI 重叠窄，论文不能写 \\\"no significant difference\\\"，应写 \\\"depth signal preserved with minor degradation\\\" 或做 permutation test\\n- **temp-only 代码污染更高**：filtered 移除 23.7% vs MVP ~15%。论文需说明原因（temperature-only 保留更多分布尾部含代码 pattern）+ 附 filtered 指标作 fairness check\\n- **DivEye 引用待验证**：\\\"Basani & Chen, ICLR 2026\\\" 需在论文阶段做 reference-audit 确认真实性\\n\\n## DCOL 训练技术经验（2026-05-14）\\n- **特征值极端**：var_ppl 可达 ~1e11 → 必须 log1p 变换 + BatchNorm1d，否则 fp16 overflow\\n- **fp16 注意**：移除 GradScaler，改用 autocast forward + fp32 loss 计算\\n- **AdamW eps**：DeBERTa multi-group optimizer 下 eps=1e-8 会产生 NaN → 改为 eps=1e-6\\n- **数据泄漏**：train/test 2-way split 用 test 做 early stopping = 上偏 → 必须 3-way split（train 70% / val 15% / test 15%）\\n\\n## 经验教训\\n\\n- 代码占比高的模型不能用于递归生成实验\\n- 域一致性检查是必做步骤\\n- 控制实验验证信号真实性：过滤代码后 acc 提升 → 域混淆排除\\n- AutoDL: HF 下载慢用 ModelScope CDN\\n- Worker 汇报需验证：读 log 确认实际执行\\n- OLMo-1B 必须用 HF 格式（allenai/OLMo-1B-hf），原始格式需自定义代码\\n- GPT-2 XL 的 attention 用 Conv1D，LoRA target 是 c_attn/c_proj\\n- **Pipeline 脚本提交前必须 `bash -n script.sh` 验证语法**（OLMo pipeline 因未闭合引号崩溃，浪费一个 GPU cycle）\\n- OLMo LoRA targets 是三个模块 (q_proj, k_proj, v_proj)，不是两个\\n- **Registry/cron server 名必须用系统已注册名称**（`gpu-6000pro-2` 或 `autodl-rtx6000`）。`westb-52439` 不在系统列表中 → cron SSH 失败 → 误杀实验（2026-05-14 OLMo 事故）\\n- **修改 registry spec.server 后必须检查 cron 是否受影响**——cron 依赖 server 字段做 SSH 连接\\n\", \"total_chars\": 3501, \"truncated\": overflow\\\\n- **fp16 注意**：移除 GradScaler，改用 autocast forward + fp32 loss 计算\\\\n- **AdamW eps**：DeBERTa multi-group optimizer 下 eps=1e-8 会产生 NaN → 改为 eps=1e-6\\\\n- **数据泄漏**：train/test 2-way split 用 test 做 early stopping = 上偏 → 必须 3-way split（train 70% / val 15% / test 15%）\\\\n\\\\n## 经验教训\\\\n\\\\n- 代码占比高的模型不能用于递归生成实验\\\\n- 域一致性检查是必做步骤\\\\n- 控制实验验证信号真实性：过滤代码后 acc 提升 → 域混淆排除\\\\n- AutoDL: HF {"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_2/projects/recursive_gen_depth_est/decisions.yaml\", \"content\": \"_next_id: 3\\ndecisions:\\n- id: D001\\n  date: 2026-05-14 14:03 UTC\\n  summary: MVP PASS — 递归生成深度可从统计特征高精度区分，进入 research phase\\n  rationale: Pythia-1.4B 4-depth pipeline 验证核心假设：4-class acc 78.2% (>>40% threshold),\\n    d1v2 AUC 0.861 (>>0.6 threshold)。控制实验排除域混淆（过滤代码后 acc 提升至 81.6%）。Top 特征为 surprisal\\n    variance 和 perplexity percentiles，说明信号来自分布窄化而非内容差异。\\n  related_experiments:\\n  - exp_id: mvp_pipeline_pythia_002\\n    role: primary\\n    result: 4-class acc 78.2%, d1v2 AUC 0.861, content-type control 81.6%\\n  depends_on: []\\n- id: D002\\n  date: 2026-05-14 16:01 UTC\\n  summary: Dream 三档分析完成：6项结构性必修+framing重心从DCOL转向Atlas/EDD\\n  rationale: Dream dream_20260514T140607 full review 完成。三档(best=borderline accept\\n    3.0, median=Findings 3.0, worst=borderline Findings 2.5)一致指出6项结构性问题：(1)DCOL loss\\n    Eq.1缺repulsive term不可复现 (2)Atlas论文零图不可接受 (3)bibliography严重不足(3-10 refs,需25+) (4)d0\\n    confound膨胀headline metrics,需报d1-d5 acc (5)EDD threshold无sensitivity analysis (6)surprisal\\n    reference model未指定有circularity风险。三档均认为Atlas/EDD是primary contribution,DCOL incremental\\n    over CLOC——与我们的contingency plan方向一致。worst case下DCOL不显著(p=0.12),EDD=2,但任务formalization+Atlas仍可支撑Findings。关键风险：cross-model\\n    transfer在worst case降到33%。\\n  related_experiments:\\n  - exp_id: dream_20260514T140607\\n    role: dream_simulation\\n    result: best=3.0 median=3.0 worst=2.5\\n  depends_on: []\\n\", \"total_chars\": 1351, \"truncated\": false}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"}

```text
I found relevant information about the DivEye paper. Here are the search results:

## Overview

DivEye is a novel detection framework that captures how unpredictability fluctuates across a text using surprisal-based features. The paper is authored by Advik Raj Basani and Pin-Yu Chen.

## Key Findings

Motivated by the observation that human-authored text exhibits richer variability in lexical and structural unpredictability than LLM outputs, DivEye captures this signal through a set of interpretable statistical features.

The method outperforms existing zero-shot detectors by up to 33.2% and achieves competitive performance with fine-tuned baselines across multiple benchmarks. Additionally, DivEye is robust to paraphrasing and adversarial attacks, generalizes well across domains and models, and improves the performance of existing detectors by up to 18.7% when used as an auxiliary signal.

## Publication Status

Regarding ICLR 2026: The paper was submitted to ICLR 2026 Conference as a Withdrawn Submission. However, the paper was accepted at TMLR '26 and ICML, Data in Generative Models (DIG-BUG) '25.

The research addresses the practical challenge of detecting AI-generated text and provides an interpretable approach that can be deployed without requiring access to the original generative model.


REMINDER: You MUST include the sources above in your response to the user using markdown hyperlinks.
```

```text
Let me review both scripts:

**OLMo temp09 script** (`run_pipeline_olmo_temp09.sh`):
1. ✅ `set -e` — fail on error
2. ✅ `GPU=${GPU:-0}` — parameterized GPU, default 0
3. ✅ `DATA_DIR="data/olmo_temp09"` — separate from nucleus (`data/olmo/`)


4. ✅ `CKPT_DIR="checkpoints/olmo_temp09"` — separate from nucleus
5. ✅ `LOG_FILE="logs/pipeline_olmo_temp09.log"` — separate log
6. ✅ Environment setup (network_turbo, HF_HOME, SSL certs, conda)
7. ✅ `MODEL_PATH="/root/autodl-tmp/models/olmo-1b-hf"` — correct
8. ✅ `TARGET_MODULES="q_proj,k_proj,v_proj"` — correct (3 modules)
9. ✅ Redirecting output to log file...
10. ✅ Decoding: `--decoding_strategy temp_only --temperature 0.9` — correct
11. ✅ 6 steps (3 finetune + 3 generate) for depth-0 through depth-3
12. ✅ All quotes properly matched

Wait, I'm noticing something important here. The first finetune step pulls from `data/olmo_temp09/depth_0.jsonl`, but depth-0 is the original human data — it shouldn't vary based on decoding strategy. The temperature 0.9 strategy only affects the generation steps, not the initial human data input.

So either the script needs to point to the shared `data/depth_0.jsonl` for that first step, or the depth-0 file needs to be copied or symlinked into the strategy-specific directory. If it's not there, Step 1 will fail. Let me check what the original OLMo pipeline does to see how this should be handled. Looking more carefully at the original scripts, the depth-0 data is actually shared across all models — both OLMo and Pythia use `data/depth_0.jsonl`, not model-specific versions. So the bug is real: the new script should reference the shared depth-0 file rather than looking for it in the model-specific data directory. Looking at the pipeline scripts, it seems depth-0 data is actually model-specific — Pythia uses `data/depth_0.jsonl`, GPT-2 XL uses `data/gpt2xl/depth_0.jsonl`, and OLMo likely follows a similar pattern with its own directory. So the issue is that the new strategy-specific scripts are looking for depth-0 files in their respective directories, but those files may not exist unless they're copied there beforehand. The scripts will fail without the depth-0 data files in place, so those need to be symlinked or copied to the strategy-specific directories. I'm realizing that the LoRA training itself is actually model-specific but strategy-independent — the same adapter could theoretically be trained once and reused across all three strategies, with only the generation steps differing. That would be more efficient than training the same LoRA three times on identical data, though it's not a correctness issue. The real blocker is making sure the depth-0 data exists where the scripts expect it.
```

[WRITE HANDOFF] 请立即写你的 handoff。

**格式硬约束**:
- 整条回复**只能**是 &lt;handoff>...&lt;/handoff> 标签包裹
- 禁止在标签外写任何寒暄 / 说明 / 确认（"好的"/"我开始写"/"写完了"等）

**必须覆盖以下 5 个 section（缺一不可，无内容写 N/A）**:

### 1. 🔴 硬约束 / 红线
  本项目特定的不可违反约束（GPU 设备号、服务器名、账号、路径限制等）。
  写本项目特定的，不要抄 CLAUDE.md 通用 L1 红线。
  下一个"你"第一眼看到的就是这里，写错了会被 Director CRITICAL。

### 2. 🖥️ 基础设施快照
  - 服务器名 + 工作目录 + Python 路径 + conda env
  - 模型路径 / 数据集路径 / wandb config / HF cache
  - 活跃进程：PID + tmux session + cron 任务 ID
  写一次就稳定，下一个"你"不用再 SSH 探查。

### 3. 📊 当前状态
  - 你刚做完什么 / 正在做什么
  - registry 里 RUNNING/PREPARING 的实验列表 + 一句话摘要
  - 等待中的 Worker 回调 / Cron / Director 决策

### 4. 📋 待办（条件触发式）
  格式 "条件 X 满足 → 动作 Y"。例如:
  - "Falcon-H1R 下载完成 → sed 修 MODEL_PATH → dry-run → 提交"
  - "pilot_011 出 GC4 结果 → ≥2pp 转架构 narrative，<2pp 写 null-result"

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
  - 项目核心结论 / 决策链（D001..）
  - 下一个"你"最容易判断错的事 / 容易踩的坑

**写作原则**：
- 第 1 节必须是项目特定的（不是通用 L1 红线）
- 第 2 节让下一轮 BOOT 后能立即开工，避免重新 SSH 探查
- 简洁 > 完整：每节 5-10 行足够，长 handoff 不等于好 handoff

示例格式:
&lt;handoff>
### 1. 🔴 硬约束 / 红线
- 本项目只能用 cuda:4 / cuda:5（之前两次违规 cuda:0 被 Director CRITICAL）
- submit_training_job 不支持 shell builtin（source/conda activate 全不行）

### 2. 🖥️ 基础设施快照
- 服务器：westd-8x6000，工作目录 /root/autodl-tmp/hybrid_reasoning_phases
- Python：/root/miniconda3/bin/python（绝对路径，不走 conda activate）
- 模型：/root/autodl-tmp/qwen35_4b_direct
- 活跃：pilot_011 (PID 203221, tmux=pilot_011, cuda:4)

### 3. 📊 当前状态
我刚完成 pilot_010 分析，正在等 pilot_011 (Falcon-H1R) CoT 生成完成（6/50）。
registry: pilot_011 RUNNING；其他全 DONE。

### 4. 📋 待办
- pilot_011 cron 回调 → spawn worker 跑 probe + GC4 计算
- GC4 ≥2pp → 转架构 narrative；<2pp → null-result paper outline 已就绪

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
Falcon-H1R 是最后一个数据点。已知 4B/9B GC4 都 FAIL。
易错：不要把 GC4-par (Falcon parallel) 和 GC4-seq (Qwen sequential) 直接对比。
&lt;/handoff>
